```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))**2 for non-negative a.

Strategy:
- Start from the provided parent construction when available.
- Use several complementary optimizers:
  * L-BFGS-B on a soft-max smoothed objective in log-simplex coordinates.
  * Exact coordinate descent with golden-section 1D searches.
  * LP fictitious play for best responses of the bilinear cross-convolution.
  * Subgradient fallbacks and small restarts.
  * Convex-pool meta-optimization: mix diverse candidate sequences and optimize
    the mixing weights.
  * Multi-length exploration (parent length, longer interpolated lengths).
- Always keep both a fast internal score and the true evaluate_sequence score.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
from scipy.sparse import csc_matrix
import scipy.optimize as spopt


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Globals for state shared by top-level helper functions.
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

_LP_CACHE = {}
_LP_MODE = [0]  # 0 auto, 1 scipy, 3 disabled
_LB_STATE = [0.0, 0]  # beta, n for L-BFGS objective/gradient


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic utilities
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def fast_autoconv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    fft_size = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = rfft(a, fft_size)
    out = irfft(A * A, fft_size)
    return out[:size]


def fast_conv_pair(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    m = len(b)
    size = n + m - 1
    fft_size = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = rfft(a, fft_size)
    B = rfft(b, fft_size)
    out = irfft(A * B, fft_size)
    return out[:size]


def quick_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = fast_autoconv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def trim_if_better(a):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 2:
        return a
    orig_score = quick_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(orig_score):
        return a
    cur = a
    cur_score = orig_score
    maxval = float(cur.max())
    if maxval <= 0.0:
        return cur
    eps = 1e-12 * maxval + 1e-15

    for _ in range(20):
        lo = 0
        hi = len(cur)
        while lo < hi and cur[lo] < eps:
            lo += 1
        while hi > lo and cur[hi - 1] < eps:
            hi -= 1
        if lo == 0 and hi == len(cur):
            break
        b = cur[lo:hi]
        if b.size < 2:
            break
        b = normalize_positive(b)
        sc = quick_score(b)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-13:
            cur = b
            cur_score = sc
        else:
            break
    return cur


def interpolate_seq(a, new_n):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    old_n = len(a)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if old_n <= 1:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    return b / s


def simplex_project(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * rho > (cssv - 1.0)
    if np.any(cond):
        idx = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
        theta = (cssv[idx] - 1.0) / (idx + 1.0)
        return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def add_normalized(inits, a):
    b = normalize_positive(a)
    if b.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        inits.append(b)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initializations
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def init_flat_spectrum(n, rng):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    n_fft = 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()
    n_freq = n_fft // 2 + 1
    phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, n_freq)
    amps = rng.uniform(0.55, 1.45, n_freq)
    spec = amps * np.exp(1j * phases)
    a = irfft(spec, n_fft)
    a = np.abs(a[:n])
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def make_inits(n, parent, rng):
    inits = []
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        inits.append(np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64))
        return inits

    if parent is not None:
        p = interpolate_seq(parent, n)
        add_normalized(inits, p)
        add_normalized(inits, p[::-1])

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    for lam in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.3, 1.6, 2.2, 3.0, 4.5):
        a = np.exp(-lam * t)
        add_normalized(inits, a)
        add_normalized(inits, a[::-1])

    for p in (1.2, 1.8, 2.5, 3.5, 5.0):
        a = (1.0 + t) ** (-p)
        add_normalized(inits, a)
        add_normalized(inits, a[::-1])

    for _ in range(2):
        add_normalized(inits, init_flat_spectrum(n, rng))

    for _ in range(2):
        add_normalized(inits, rng.exponential(1.0, n))
        add_normalized(inits, rng.random(n))

    for _ in range(3):
        a = np.sort(rng.exponential(1.0, n))[::-1]
        add_normalized(inits, a)
        add_normalized(inits, a[::-1])

    for center in (0.18, 0.25, 0.33, 0.42, 0.5):
        for width in (0.07, 0.12, 0.20):
            a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - center) / width) ** 2) + 1e-8
            add_normalized(inits, a)
            add_normalized(inits, a[::-1])

    a = 1.0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n)
    add_normalized(inits, a)

    if not inits:
        inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    return inits


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# L-BFGS-B on smoothed objective in log-simplex coordinates
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def softmax_from_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.zeros_like(y)
    z = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        z = z - float(np.max(z))
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def lbfgs_objective(y):
    beta = float(_LB_STATE[0])
    n = int(_LB_STATE[1])
    a = softmax_from_y(y)
    c = fast_autoconv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if beta <= 1e-3 or c.size == 0:
        M = cmax
    else:
        e = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
        z = float(e.sum())
        if z <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(z):
            M = cmax
        else:
            M = cmax + float(np.log(z)) / beta
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        M = cmax
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def lbfgs_gradient(y):
    beta = float(_LB_STATE[0])
    n = int(_LB_STATE[1])
    a = softmax_from_y(y)
    c = fast_autoconv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 1e-3:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        M = cmax
    else:
        e = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
        z = float(e.sum())
        if z <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(z):
            w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            M = cmax
        else:
            w = e / z
            M = cmax + float(np.log(z)) / beta
    h = weighted_shift(a, w)
    g = 2.0 * float(n) * (h - a * M)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return g


def weighted_shift(a, w):
    """
    Return h where h_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i} for valid indices.
    This is the vector A_w a for the weighted average of convolution shifts.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    m = len(w)
    size = n + m - 1
    fft_size = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    R = w[::-1]
    A = rfft(a, fft_size)
    B = rfft(R, fft_size)
    conv = irfft(A * B, fft_size)
    # h_i = conv[2n-2-i] for i=0..n-1
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1].copy()


def l_bfgs_polish(a0, time_limit, beta_targets=(2.0, 6.0, 12.0), label=""):
    cur = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(cur)
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 5.0:
        return cur, quick_score(cur)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    best = cur.copy()
    best_sc = quick_score(cur)

    eps = 1e-12 / float(n)
    low = float(math.log(eps))
    high = 0.0
    bounds = [(low, high)] * n

    safe = np.maximum(cur, eps)
    safe = safe / safe.sum()
    y0 = np.log(safe)

    for target in beta_targets:
        rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        cmax = float(fast_autoconv(cur).max())
        beta = float(target) / max(cmax, 1e-12)
        beta = min(100000.0, max(1.0, beta))
        _LB_STATE[0] = float(beta)
        _LB_STATE[1] = int(n)

        if n >= 1024:
            maxfun = 450
            maxiter = 120
        else:
            maxfun = 900
            maxiter = 250

        try:
            res = spopt.minimize(
                lbfgs_objective,
                y0,
                jac=lbfgs_gradient,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
            if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                y_new = np.clip(res.x, low, high)
                a_new = softmax_from_y(y_new)
                a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
                ss = float(a_new.sum())
                if ss > 1e-300:
                    a_new = a_new / ss
                    sc = quick_score(a_new)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                        best = a_new.copy()
                        best_sc = sc
                        cur = a_new.copy()
                        if label:
                            print(f"    LBFGS {label}: {best_sc:.8f} beta={beta:.0f}")
                    else:
                        cur_sc = quick_score(cur)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc:
                            cur = a_new.copy()

                safe = np.maximum(cur, eps)
                safe = safe / safe.sum()
                y0 = np.log(safe)
        except Exception:
            pass

    best = trim_if_better(best)
    best_sc = quick_score(best)
    return best, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Subgradient fallback
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def subgradient_candidate(a, lrs=(0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40)):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2:
        return a, quick_score(a)
    c = fast_autoconv(a)
    K = min(32, c.size)
    if K <= 0:
        return a, quick_score(a)
    peaks = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    w[peaks] = 1.0 / float(len(peaks))
    h = weighted_shift(a, w)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = quick_score(a)
    for lr in lrs:
        cand = simplex_project(a - float(lr) * h)
        sc = quick_score(cand)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc
    return best, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Line search for convex combinations
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def line_search_combo(u, v, c_u, c_v, c_uv, n):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)

    full_t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 129)
    T = full_t[:, None]
    c = (
        (1.0 - T) ** 2 * c_u[None, :]
        + (2.0 * T * (1.0 - T)) * c_uv[None, :]
        + (T ** 2) * c_v[None, :]
    )
    scores = 2.0 * float(n) * c.max(axis=1)
    idx = int(np.argmin(scores))
    best_t = float(full_t[idx])
    best_sc = float(scores[idx])

    # Local refinements around a few good samples.
    top_idx = np.argsort(scores)[:5]
    for ti in top_idx:
        t0 = float(full_t[ti])
        lo = max(0.0, t0 - 0.012)
        hi = min(1.0, t0 + 0.012)
        if hi <= lo + 1e-15:
            continue
        t2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 33)
        T2 = t2[:, None]
        c2 = (
            (1.0 - T2) ** 2 * c_u[None, :]
            + (2.0 * T2 * (1.0 - T2)) * c_uv[None, :]
            + (T2 ** 2) * c_v[None, :]
        )
        sc2 = 2.0 * float(n) * c2.max(axis=1)
        j = int(np.argmin(sc2))
        t3 = float(t2[j])
        s3 = float(sc2[j])
        if s3 < best_sc:
            best_sc = s3
            best_t = t3

    cand = (1.0 - best_t) * u + best_t * v
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
    s = float(cand.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        cand = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        cand = cand / s
    return best_t, cand, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# LP best response (scipy HiGHS only, with time limit)
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def get_lp_structure(n):
    if n in _LP_CACHE:
        return _LP_CACHE[n]
    if len(_LP_CACHE) >= 3:
        _LP_CACHE.clear()

    m = 2 * n - 1

    # T[k,j] = a[j] if 0 <= k-j < n.
    T_indices = (np.tile(np.arange(n), n) + np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)).astype(np.int32)
    T_indptr = (np.arange(n + 1, dtype=np.int64) * n)

    A_indices = np.concatenate((T_indices, np.arange(m, dtype=np.int32)))
    A_indptr = np.concatenate(
        (T_indptr, np.array([n * n + m], dtype=np.int64))
    )

    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)] + [(-np.inf, np.inf)]

    st = {
        "n": n,
        "m": m,
        "T_indices": T_indices,
        "T_indptr": T_indptr,
        "A_indices": A_indices,
        "A_indptr": A_indptr,
        "A_eq": A_eq,
        "b_eq": b_eq,
        "c": c_obj,
        "bounds": bounds,
    }
    _LP_CACHE[n] = st
    return st


def lp_best_response(a, timeout_sec=5.0):
    """
    Solve:
      min t
      s.t. conv(a,b)_k <= t for all k,
           sum(b)=1, b>=0.
    Returns (b, t). If unavailable, returns (None, inf).
    """
    if _LP_MODE[0] == 3:
        return None, float("inf")

    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    st = get_lp_structure(n)

    try:
        T_data = np.repeat(a, n)
        data = np.concatenate((T_data, -np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)))
        A_ub = csc_matrix(
            (data, st["A_indices"], st["A_indptr"]),
            shape=(m, n + 1),
        )
        options = {"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(max(1.0, timeout_sec))}
        res = spopt.linprog(
            st["c"],
            A_ub=A_ub,
            A_eq=st["A_eq"],
            b_ub=np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64),
            b_eq=st["b_eq"],
            bounds=st["bounds"],
            method="highs",
            options=options,
        )
        if res.x is not None:
            b = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
            s = float(b.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                b = b / s
                tval = float(res.x[-1]) if np.isfinite(res.x[-1]) else float("inf")
                _LP_MODE[0] = 1
                return b, tval
        _LP_MODE[0] = 3
    except Exception:
        _LP_MODE[0] = 3

    return None, float("inf")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fictitious play with LP best responses
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def add_candidate(pool, cand, max_size=35):
    if pool is None:
        return
    cand = normalize_positive(cand)
    if cand.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
        return
    sc = quick_score(cand)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    pool.append(cand)
    if len(pool) > max_size:
        scored = []
        for i, x in enumerate(pool):
            sx = quick_score(x)
            scored.append((sx, i, x))
        scored.sort()
        keep = scored[: max(1, max_size // 2)]
        del pool[:]
        for _, _, x in keep:
            pool.append(x)


def fictitious_play(a_start, budget, pool=None, label=""):
    a = normalize_positive(a_start)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2:
        return a, quick_score(a), a

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = quick_score(a)
    active = a.copy()
    avg = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_lp = 0

    # For very large n, LP cost/memory is too high; use subgradient moves only.
    lp_allowed = n <= 1600

    if pool is not None:
        add_candidate(pool, best)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget:
        rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        it += 1
        if it > 20000:
            break

        if not lp_allowed or _LP_MODE[0] == 3:
            active, sc = subgradient_candidate(active)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best = active.copy()
                best_sc = sc
            if it >= 300:
                break
            continue

        lp_timeout = min(6.0, max(2.0, rem / 10.0))
        b, _ = lp_best_response(active, timeout_sec=lp_timeout)

        if b is None:
            no_lp += 1
            if no_lp >= 4:
                break
            active, sc = subgradient_candidate(active)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best = active.copy()
                best_sc = sc
            continue

        no_lp = 0

        c_active = fast_autoconv(active)
        c_b = fast_autoconv(b)
        c_cross = fast_conv_pair(active, b)
        _t1, cand1, sc1 = line_search_combo(active, b, c_active, c_b, c_cross, n)

        did2 = (it % 2 == 0)
        sc2 = float("inf")
        cand2 = cand1
        if did2:
            c_best = fast_autoconv(best)
            c_cross_best = fast_conv_pair(best, b)
            _t2, cand2, sc2 = line_search_combo(best, b, c_best, c_b, c_cross_best, n)

        if sc1 < best_sc:
            best = cand1.copy()
            best_sc = sc1
        if did2 and sc2 < best_sc:
            best = cand2.copy()
            best_sc = sc2

        alpha = 2.0 / float(it + 3)
        mix = (1.0 - alpha) * avg + alpha * b
        mix = normalize_positive(mix)
        sc_mix = quick_score(mix)
        if sc_mix < best_sc:
            best = mix.copy()
            best_sc = sc_mix

        active_score = sc1
        active_arr = cand1
        if sc_mix < active_score:
            active_score = sc_mix
            active_arr = mix
        if best_sc < active_score:
            active_score = best_sc
            active_arr = best
        if did2 and sc2 < active_score:
            active_score = sc2
            active_arr = cand2

        active = active_arr.copy()
        avg = mix

        if pool is not None and it % 8 == 0:
            add_candidate(pool, active)
            add_candidate(pool, mix)
            add_candidate(pool, b)

        if label and it % 25 == 0:
            print(f"    FP {label}: it={it} score={best_sc:.8f}")

    return best, best_sc, avg


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Exact coordinate descent
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def coord_score(x, params):
    c_lin, ap, has_quad, c_quad, unaff, S, v, n = params
    ns = S + x - v
    if ns < 1e-12:
        return 1e100
    m = unaff
    if c_lin.size > 0:
        val = float(np.max(c_lin + 2.0 * ap * x))
        if val > m:
            m = val
    if has_quad:
        q = c_quad + x * x
        if q > m:
            m = q
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (ns * ns))


def golden_section_params(lo, hi, max_iter, params):
    if hi <= lo:
        return float(lo)
    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    c = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
    d = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
    fc = coord_score(c, params)
    fd = coord_score(d, params)
    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if hi - lo < 1e-14 * max(1.0, abs(c)):
            break
        if fc <= fd:
            hi = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
            fc = coord_score(c, params)
        else:
            lo = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
            fd = coord_score(d, params)
    return 0.5 * (lo + hi)


def coord_prep(a, conv, S, i):
    n = len(a)
    v = float(a[i])
    k_lo = int(i)
    k_hi = min(i + n - 1, 2 * n - 2)
    n_aff = k_hi - k_lo + 1

    seg = conv[k_lo:k_hi + 1]
    ip = np.arange(n_aff, dtype=np.int64)
    ap = ip.astype(np.float64)
    c_lin = seg - 2.0 * v * a[ip]

    has_quad = (2 * i >= k_lo) and (2 * i <= k_hi)
    c_quad = 0.0
    qidx = -1
    if has_quad:
        qidx = int(2 * i - k_lo)
        c_quad = float(conv[int(2 * i)]) - v * v
        c_lin = c_lin.copy()
        ap = ap.copy()
        c_lin[qidx] = -1e100
        ap[qidx] = 0.0

    unaff = -1e30
    if k_lo > 0:
        val = float(conv[:k_lo].max())
        if val > unaff:
            unaff = val
    if k_hi < 2 * n - 2:
        val = float(conv[k_hi + 1:].max())
        if val > unaff:
            unaff = val

    params = (c_lin, ap, has_quad, c_quad, unaff, float(S), v, int(n))
    return params, k_lo, k_hi, has_quad, qidx


def exact_coord_descent(a0, time_limit, rng=None, label=""):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 1.0:
        return a, quick_score(a)

    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)

    conv = fast_autoconv(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    cur_sc = quick_score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur_sc

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    sweeps = 0
    no_improve = 0
    max_iter = 22 if n > 1024 else 30

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        sweeps += 1
        perm = rng.permutation(n)
        improved = False

        for i in perm:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_limit:
                break
            v = float(a[i])

            if v < 1e-11:
                # Occasionally let a zero coordinate grow.
                if rng.random() < 0.04:
                    upper = 10.0 / float(n)
                else:
                    continue
            else:
                upper = max(5.0 * v, 10.0 / float(n))
                if upper > 1.0:
                    upper = 1.0

            if upper <= 1e-18 or upper <= v * 1e-12:
                continue

            params, k_lo, k_hi, has_quad, qidx = coord_prep(a, conv, S, i)
            best_x = golden_section_params(0.0, upper, max_iter, params)
            new_sc = coord_score(best_x, params)

            if np.isfinite(new_sc) and new_sc < cur_sc - 1e-15:
                delta = best_x - v
                a[i] = best_x
                S += delta

                if S < 1e-6:
                    a = normalize_positive(a)
                    S = float(a.sum())
                    conv = fast_autoconv(a)
                    cur_sc = quick_score(a)
                    if cur_sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = cur_sc
                        best = a.copy()
                    break

                ip_update = np.arange(k_lo, k_hi + 1) - i
                changes = 2.0 * delta * a[ip_update]
                if has_quad:
                    changes = changes.copy()
                    changes[qidx] = delta * (2.0 * v + delta)
                conv[k_lo:k_hi + 1] += changes

                cur_sc = new_sc
                if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_sc = cur_sc
                    best = a.copy()
                improved = True

        if sweeps % 5 == 0:
            a = normalize_positive(a)
            S = float(a.sum())
            conv = fast_autoconv(a)
            cur_sc = quick_score(a)
            if cur_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = cur_sc
                best = a.copy()

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                break
        else:
            no_improve = 0

        if label and sweeps % 25 == 0:
            print(f"    ECD {label}: sweep={sweeps} score={best_sc:.8f}")

    final_sc = quick_score(best)
    return best, final_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Meta-optimization over a pool of candidate sequences
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def optimize_weights(pool, n, budget, label=""):
    if not pool:
        return None, float("inf")

    arrs = []
    for a in pool[:40]:
        b = interpolate_seq(a, n)
        arrs.append(b)

    scored = []
    for i, a in enumerate(arrs):
        sc = quick_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, i, a))
    if not scored:
        return None, float("inf")

    scored.sort()
    picked = []
    for sc, idx, a in scored:
        if len(picked) >= 10:
            break
        diverse = True
        for b in picked:
            if float(np.dot(a, b)) > 0.995:
                diverse = False
                break
        if diverse:
            picked.append(a)

    P = len(picked)
    if P == 1:
        return picked[0], quick_score(picked[0])

    A = np.stack(picked, axis=0)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    C = np.zeros((P, P, m), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(P):
        for j in range(i, P):
            cij = fast_conv_pair(A[i], A[j])
            C[i, j] = cij
            C[j, i] = cij

    lam = np.ones(P, dtype=np.float64) / float(P)
    best_lam = lam.copy()
    best_a = normalize_positive(A @ lam)
    best_sc = quick_score(best_a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    sweeps = 0
    no_improve = 0
    factors = (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget:
        sweeps += 1
        improved = False

        for i in range(P):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget:
                break
            base = lam
            for f in factors:
                cand = base.copy()
                cand[i] = base[i] * float(f)
                ss = float(cand.sum())
                if ss < 1e-12:
                    continue
                cand /= ss
                c_out = (C @ cand) @ cand
                sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c_out.max())
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_lam = cand.copy()
                    best_a = normalize_positive(A @ cand)
                    lam = cand.copy()
                    base = cand
                    improved = True

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 4:
                break
        else:
            no_improve = 0

    if label:
        print(f"    WEIGHTS {label}: {best_sc:.8f}")
    return best_a, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Small-n global seed search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def small_n_search(n, budget, rng):
    if budget < 12.0:
        return None
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    inits = make_inits(n, None, rng)
    scored = []
    for i, a in enumerate(inits):
        sc = quick_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, i, a))
    if not scored:
        return None
    scored.sort()

    best_a = scored[0][2]
    best_sc = scored[0][0]

    for pos in range(1, min(4, len(scored))):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget * 0.75:
            break
        a_c = scored[pos][2]

        b, sb = exact_coord_descent(a_c, max(3.0, min(8.0, budget / 4.0)), rng)
        if sb < best_sc:
            best_a, best_sc = b, sb

        b, sb = l_bfgs_polish(b, max(3.0, min(12.0, budget / 3.0)), beta_targets=(4.0, 10.0))
        if sb < best_sc:
            best_a, best_sc = b, sb

        b, sb = exact_coord_descent(b, max(3.0, min(12.0, budget / 3.0)), rng)
        if sb < best_sc:
            best_a, best_sc = b, sb

    return best_a, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# General polish and basin hopping
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def polish_sequence(a0, budget, rng, label=""):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    sc = quick_score(a)
    if budget < 20.0:
        return a, sc

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    lb_b = min(budget * 0.45, 220.0)
    if lb_b >= 12.0:
        a, sc = l_bfgs_polish(a, lb_b, beta_targets=(4.0, 10.0, 20.0), label=label)

    rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 15.0:
        a2, sc2 = exact_coord_descent(a, rem, rng, label=label)
        if sc2 < sc:
            a, sc = a2, sc2
    return a, sc


def basin_hopping(a0, budget, rng, pool=None, label=""):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2 or budget < 45.0:
        return a, quick_score(a)

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = quick_score(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    sigmas = (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.03, 0.05, 0.08)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget:
        rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 55.0:
            break
        it += 1
        pol_b = min(65.0, max(25.0, rem * 0.25))
        r = float(rng.random())

        if r < 0.4:
            sigma = float(sigmas[int(rng.integers(0, len(sigmas)))])
            pert = a + rng.standard_normal(n) * sigma
        elif r < 0.7 and pool is not None and len(pool) > 0:
            other = pool[int(rng.integers(0, len(pool)))]
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
            pert = alpha * a + (1.0 - alpha) * interpolate_seq(other, n)
        else:
            conv = fast_autoconv(a)
            K = min(10, conv.size)
            peaks = np.argpartition(conv, -K)[-K:]
            pert = a.copy()
            for k in peaks:
                k = int(k)
                i_st = max(0, k - n + 1)
                i_en = min(n - 1, k)
                idxs = np.arange(i_st, i_en + 1)
                if len(idxs) == 0:
                    continue
                take = max(1, len(idxs) // 5)
                picks = rng.choice(idxs, size=min(take, len(idxs)), replace=False)
                for ii in picks:
                    pert[int(ii)] *= (1.0 + rng.standard_normal() * 0.10)

        pert = np.maximum(pert, 0.0)
        s = float(pert.sum())
        if s < 1e-12:
            continue
        pert = pert / s

        b, sb = l_bfgs_polish(pert, pol_b, beta_targets=(5.0, 12.0), label=label)
        if sb < best_sc:
            best = b.copy()
            best_sc = sb

        rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem > 30.0:
            b2, sb2 = exact_coord_descent(best, min(35.0, rem * 0.5), rng, label=label)
            if sb2 < best_sc:
                best = b2.copy()
                best_sc = sb2

    return best, best_sc


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Score bookkeeping helper
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def update_scores(search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, cand, label=""):
    if cand is None:
        return search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual

    cand = normalize_positive(cand)
    cand = trim_if_better(cand)
    qs = quick_score(cand)
    if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < search_score - 1e-13:
        search_seq = cand.copy()
        search_score = float(qs)

    try:
        asq = float(evaluate_sequence(cand.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        asq = float("inf")

    if np.isfinite(asq) and asq < best_actual - 1e-13:
        best_seq = cand.copy()
        best_actual = float(asq)
        search_seq = best_seq.copy()
        if np.isfinite(qs):
            search_score = float(qs)
        if label:
            print(f"  ACTUAL UPDATE [{label}]: {best_actual:.8f} (n={len(best_seq)})")
    elif label:
        print(f"  no actual update [{label}]: actual={asq:.8f}, quick={qs:.8f}, best_actual={best_actual:.8f}")

    return search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate():
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 1780.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240528)

    search_seq = None
    search_score = float("inf")
    best_seq = None
    best_actual = float("inf")

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.isfinite(p).all() and float(p.sum()) > 1e-6:
            parent = normalize_positive(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        # Do not search with extremely long parents.
        if len(parent) > 2048:
            parent = interpolate_seq(parent, 2048)
            parent = trim_if_better(parent)
        parent = trim_if_better(parent)
        search_seq = parent.copy()
        search_score = quick_score(search_seq)
        best_seq = parent.copy()
        try:
            best_actual = float(evaluate_sequence(best_seq.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            best_actual = float("inf")
        n0 = len(search_seq)
        if n0 < 512:
            n0 = 1024
            interp = interpolate_seq(search_seq, n0)
            interp = trim_if_better(interp)
            interp_sc = quick_score(interp)
            if interp_sc < search_score:
                search_seq = interp
                search_score = interp_sc
                best_seq = interp
                try:
                    best_actual = float(evaluate_sequence(best_seq.tolist()))
                except Exception:
                    best_actual = float("inf")
        else:
            n0 = len(search_seq)
        print(f"Parent: n={len(best_seq)} quick={search_score:.8f} actual={best_actual:.8f}")
    else:
        n0 = 1024
        search_seq = np.ones(n0, dtype=np.float64) / float(n0)
        search_score = quick_score(search_seq)
        best_seq = search_seq.copy()
        try:
            best_actual = float(evaluate_sequence(best_seq.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            best_actual = float("inf")
        print(f"No parent: uniform n={n0}, actual={best_actual:.8f}")

    pool = []

    # Very short early polish to escape a trivial parent if needed.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 1250.0:
        cand, _ = polish_sequence(search_seq, min(70.0, rem - 1200.0), rng, label="early")
        search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, cand, "early"
        )

    # Small-n global seed.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 1100.0:
        small = small_n_search(96, min(45.0, rem - 1050.0), rng)
        if small is not None:
            a_s, _ = small
            cand = interpolate_seq(a_s, n0)
            cand = trim_if_better(cand)
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
                search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, cand, "small_seed"
            )
            add_candidate(pool, cand)

    # Initialization pool and quick local optimization.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 850.0:
        inits = make_inits(len(search_seq), search_seq, rng)
        scored = []
        for i, a in enumerate(inits):
            sc = quick_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, i, a))
        scored.sort()
        count = min(3, len(scored))
        per_b = min(50.0, max(15.0, (rem - 800.0) / max(1, count)))
        for pos in range(count):
            a_c = scored[pos][2]
            a_c, _ = polish_sequence(a_c, per_b, rng, label=f"init{pos}")
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
                search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_c, f"init{pos}"
            )
            add_candidate(pool, a_c)
            add_candidate(pool, a_c[::-1])

    # Main LP fictitious play at primary length.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    fp_budget = min(520.0, max(0.0, rem - 750.0))
    if fp_budget > 70.0:
        a_fp, s_fp, avg_fp = fictitious_play(
            search_seq, fp_budget, pool=pool, label=f"fp_n{len(search_seq)}"
        )
        search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_fp, "fp1"
        )
        search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, avg_fp, "fp_avg1"
        )
        add_candidate(pool, a_fp)
        add_candidate(pool, avg_fp)

    # Strong polish of current best at primary length.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    pol_budget = min(320.0, max(0.0, rem - 450.0))
    if pol_budget > 50.0:
        a_pol, _ = polish_sequence(search_seq, pol_budget, rng, label="polish1")
        search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_pol, "polish1"
        )
        add_candidate(pool, a_pol)

    # Meta-weight optimization over stored diverse candidates.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    w_budget = min(130.0, max(0.0, rem - 320.0))
    if w_budget > 40.0 and pool:
        a_w, s_w = optimize_weights(pool + [search_seq, best_seq], len(search_seq), w_budget, label="weights1")
        search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_w, "weights1"
        )
        add_candidate(pool, a_w)

    # Longer-length exploration.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 420.0:
        base_n = len(search_seq)
        n_long = int(min(2048, max(base_n + 384, base_n * 2)))
        n_long = (n_long // 64) * 64
        if n_long < 512:
            n_long = 1024
        a_long = interpolate_seq(best_seq if best_seq is not None else search_seq, n_long)
        a_long = trim_if_better(a_long)
        long_budget = min(360.0, max(0.0, rem - 300.0))
        if n_long <= 1600:
            a_l, s_l, avg_l = fictitious_play(
                a_long, long_budget, pool=pool, label=f"fp_long_n{n_long}"
            )
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
                search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_l, "long_fp"
            )
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
                search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, avg_l, "long_avg"
            )
            add_candidate(pool, a_l)
            add_candidate(pool, avg_l)
        else:
            a_l, s_l = polish_sequence(a_long, long_budget, rng, label=f"long_n{n_long}")
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
                search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_l, "long_polish"
            )
            add_candidate(pool, a_l)

        # If time remains at the long length, run one short weight pass.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 200.0 and pool:
            a_w2, s_w2 = optimize_weights(
                pool + [search_seq, best_seq], len(search_seq),
                min(80.0, rem - 160.0), label="weights_long"
            )
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
                search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_w2, "weights_long"
            )

    # Basin hopping around current search point.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    bh_budget = min(160.0, max(0.0, rem - 90.0))
    if bh_budget > 50.0:
        a_b, s_b = basin_hopping(search_seq, bh_budget, rng, pool=pool, label="basin")
        search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_b, "basin"
        )
        add_candidate(pool, a_b)

    # Final polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 45.0:
        a_f, s_f = polish_sequence(
            search_seq if search_seq is not None else best_seq,
            min(130.0, rem - 20.0),
            rng,
            label="final"
        )
        search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual = update_scores(
            search_seq, search_score, best_seq, best_actual, a_f, "final"
        )

    # Final candidate selection with the true evaluator.
    final_candidates = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        final_candidates.append(trim_if_better(best_seq))
    if search_seq is not None:
        final_candidates.append(trim_if_better(search_seq))

    # Also try zeroing tiny entries.
    extra = []
    for cand in list(final_candidates):
        c2 = cand.copy()
        c2 = np.maximum(c2, 0.0)
        c2[c2 < 1e-15] = 0.0
        c2 = normalize_positive(c2)
        extra.append(trim_if_better(c2))
    for cand in extra:
        final_candidates.append(cand)

    chosen = None
    chosen_score = float("inf")
    for cand in final_candidates:
        cand = normalize_positive(cand)
        if cand.size == 0:
            continue
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(cand.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < chosen_score:
            chosen_score = sc
            chosen = cand

    if chosen is None:
        if best_seq is not None:
            chosen = best_seq
        elif search_seq is not None:
            chosen = search_seq
        else:
            chosen = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    chosen = normalize_positive(chosen)
    chosen[np.abs(chosen) < 1e-18] = 0.0
    chosen = normalize_positive(chosen)

    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(chosen.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        final_score = float("inf")

    print(f"FINAL internal-like: {quick_score(chosen):.8f}, actual: {final_score:.8f}, n={len(chosen)}")

    return [float(x) for x in chosen]
```
